User message:
I'm pretty sure the other section in the questions that the claude code agent asks is meant to be individual to each tab of questions. That is for every section in which there is a set of options to choose from, there is an other where we just type what we want. But that should be per section. And right now there is a single other tab that carries through the several tabs of s sections of questions which is wrong.

This skill should be used when the user is looking for functionality that might exist as an installable skill.\n- implementing-react-i18n: Use when adding, fixing, or reviewing React internationalization, language selectors, locale resources, translated UI copy, or bugs where changing language does not update user-facing strings\n- improve: Survey any codebase as a senior advisor and produce prioritized, self-contained implementation plans for OTHER models/agents to execute. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by running the app and observing behavior. Use when asked to verify a PR, confirm a fix works, test a change manually, check that a feature works, or validate local changes before pushing.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). 1	"use client";
```text
2	
3	import React from "react";
4	import { Check, MessageCircleQuestion, X } from "lucide-react";
5	import { workerCardManager } from "@/components/component-state-managers";
6	import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
7	import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
8	import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
9	import {
10	  buildElicitationContent,
11	  hasInvalidElicitationField,
12	  hasMissingRequiredElicitationField,
13	  parseElicitationFields,
14	  type ElicitationValue,
15	} from "@/lib/acp/elicitation-schema";
16	import { t, useI18nSnapshot } from "@/lib/i18n";
17	import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
18	import { useManagerSnapshot } from "@/lib/use-manager-snapshot";
19	import type { PendingWorkerElicitation } from "@/interface/home/worker-elicitations";
20	
21	export type ElicitationResponseInput = {
22	  workerId: string;
23	  requestId: number;
24	  action: "accept" | "decline" | "cancel";
25	  content?: Record<string, ElicitationValue>;
26	};
27	
28	function requestDraftPrefix(workerId: string, elicitation: PendingWorkerElicitation) {
29	  return [
30	    workerId,
31	    elicitation.sessionId ?? "session",
32	    elicitation.requestId,
33	    elicitation.toolCallId ?? "tool",
34	    "",
35	  ].join(":");
36	}
37	
38	function optionDraftKey(prefix: string, fieldName: string, optionValue: string) {
39	  return `${prefix}${fieldName}:option:${optionValue}`;
40	}
41	
42	export function InlineElicitation({
43	  workerId,
44	  elicitation,
45	  onRespond,
46	  disabled = false,
47	  className,
48	}: {
49	  workerId: string;
50	  elicitation: PendingWorkerElicitation;
51	  onRespond?: (input: ElicitationResponseInput) => void | Promise<unknown>;
52	  disabled?: boolean;
53	  className?: string;
54	}) {
55	  useI18nSnapshot();
56	  useManagerSnapshot(workerCardManager);
57	  const fields = parseElicitationFields(elicitation.requestedSchema);
58	  const questionFields = fields.filter((field) => /^question_\d+$/.test(field.name));
59	  const usesQuestionTabs = questionFields.length > 1;
60	  const isUrl = elicitation.mode === "url" && Boolean(elicitation.url);
61	  const safeUrl = isUrl && /^https?:\/\//i.test(elicitation.url ?? "") ? elicitation.url : null;
62	  const prefix = requestDraftPrefix(workerId, elicitation);
63	  const activeQuestionDraftKey = `${prefix}active-question`;
64	  const storedActiveQuestion = workerCardManager.readElicitationDraft(activeQuestionDraftKey);
65	  const activeQuestion = questionFields.find((field) => field.name === storedActiveQuestion)
66	    ?? questionFields[0]
67	    ?? null;
68	  const visibleFields = usesQuestionTabs
69	    ? fields.filter((field) => !/^question_\d+$/.test(field.name) || field.name === activeQuestion?.name)
70	    : fields;
71	  const values = Object.fromEntries(fields.map((field) => {
72	    if (field.kind === "multi_select") {
73	      const defaults = Array.isArray(field.defaultValue) ? field.defaultValue : [];
74	      const selected = field.options
75	        .filter((option) => {
76	          const stored = workerCardManager.readElicitationDraft(optionDraftKey(prefix, field.name, option.value));
77	          return stored === "true" || (stored === "" && defaults.includes(option.value));
78	        })
79	        .map((option) => option.value);
80	      return [field.name, selected];
81	    }
82	    if (field.kind === "boolean") {
83	      const stored = workerCardManager.readElicitationDraft(`${prefix}${field.name}`);
84	      return [field.name, stored ? stored === "true" : field.defaultValue === true];
85	    }
86	    const stored = workerCardManager.readElicitationDraft(`${prefix}${field.name}`);
87	    return [field.name, stored !== "" ? stored : field.defaultValue ?? ""];
88	  })) as Record<string, ElicitationValue>;
89	  const missingRequired = hasMissingRequiredElicitationField(fields, values);
90	  const invalid = hasInvalidElicitationField(fields, values);
91	
92	  const submit = async (action: ElicitationResponseInput["action"]) => {
93	    try {
94	      await onRespond?.({
95	        workerId,
96	        requestId: elicitation.requestId,
97	        action,
98	        ...(action === "accept" ? { content: buildElicitationContent(fields, values) } : {}),
99	      });
100	      workerCardManager.clearElicitationDrafts(prefix);
101	    } catch {
102	      // The mutation owner restores the pending interaction and surfaces the
103	      // error; retaining the request-token draft lets the user retry safely.
104	    }
105	  };
106	
107	  return (
108	    <section
109	      className={cn(
110	        // Sits on the app's card surface like every other panel, with the sky
111	        // accent carried by a single edge rather than a full wash.
112	        "relative overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-card p-4 pl-[1.0625rem] text-card-foreground ring-1 ring-foreground/10",
113	        "before:absolute before:inset-y-0 before:left-0 before:w-[3px] before:bg-sky-500/70 dark:before:bg-sky-400/70",
114	        className,
115	      )}
116	      aria-label={t("worker.elicitation.title")}
117	    >
118	      {/* Eyebrow: quietest thing in the card. The question below is the title. */}
119	      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[0.6875rem] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-muted-foreground">
120	        <MessageCircleQuestion className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400" aria-hidden="true" />
121	        {t("worker.elicitation.title")}
122	      </div>
123	      <p className="mt-1.5 text-[0.9375rem] font-semibold leading-6 text-foreground">
124	        {elicitation.message || t("worker.elicitation.defaultQuestion")}
125	      </p>
126	      {safeUrl ? (
127	        <a
128	          href={safeUrl}
129	          target="_blank"
130	          rel="noreferrer noopener"
131	          className="mt-3 inline-flex h-9 items-center rounded-lg bg-sky-500/10 px-3 text-sm font-medium text-sky-700 transition-colors hover:bg-sky-500/15 dark:text-sky-300"
132	        >
133	          {t("worker.elicitation.openLink")}
134	        </a>
135	      ) : null}
136	      {usesQuestionTabs ? (
137	        <div
138	          role="tablist"
139	          aria-label={t("worker.elicitation.title")}
140	          className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-x-1 border-b border-border/70"
141	        >
142	          {questionFields.map((field, index) => {
143	            const selected = field.name === activeQuestion?.name;
144	            const tabId = `${prefix}${field.name}:tab`;
145	            return (
146	              <button
147	                key={field.name}
148	                id={tabId}
149	                type="button"
150	                role="tab"
151	                aria-selected={selected}
152	                tabIndex={selected ? 0 : -1}
153	                disabled={disabled}
154	                onClick={() => workerCardManager.setElicitationDraft(activeQuestionDraftKey, field.name)}
155	                onKeyDown={(event) => {
156	                  const direction = event.key === "ArrowRight" ? 1 : event.key === "ArrowLeft" ? -1 : 0;
157	                  const targetIndex = event.key === "Home"
158	                    ? 0
159	                    : event.key === "End"
160	                      ? questionFields.length - 1
161	                      : direction
162	                        ? (index + direction + questionFields.length) % questionFields.length
163	                        : -1;
164	                  const target = questionFields[targetIndex];
165	                  if (!target) return;
166	                  event.preventDefault();
167	                  workerCardManager.setElicitationDraft(activeQuestionDraftKey, target.name);
168	                  document.getElementById(`${prefix}${target.name}:tab`)?.focus();
169	                }}
170	                className={cn(
171	                  "-mb-px border-b-2 px-2.5 pb-2 pt-1 text-[0.8125rem] font-medium transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-background disabled:opacity-50",
172	                  selected
173	                    ? "border-sky-500 text-foreground dark:border-sky-400"
174	                    : "border-transparent text-muted-foreground hover:border-border hover:text-foreground",
175	                )}
176	              >
177	                {field.label}
178	              </button>
179	            );
180	          })}
181	        </div>
182	      ) : null}
183	      <div
184	        role={usesQuestionTabs ? "tabpanel" : undefined}
185	        aria-labelledby={usesQuestionTabs && activeQuestion ? `${prefix}${activeQuestion.name}:tab` : undefined}
186	        className="mt-4 space-y-4"
187	      >
188	        {visibleFields.map((field) => {
189	          const draftKey = `${prefix}${field.name}`;
190	          const value = values[field.name];
191	          const isTabbedQuestion = usesQuestionTabs && /^question_\d+$/.test(field.name);
192	          return (
193	            <fieldset key={field.name} className="space-y-1.5">
194	              <legend className={cn("text-[0.8125rem] font-medium text-foreground", isTabbedQuestion && "sr-only")}>{field.label}</legend>
195	              {field.description ? (
196	                <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">{field.description}</p>
197	              ) : null}
198	              {field.kind === "single_select" ? (
199	                // A bordered, shaded box per option turned a short list of words
200	                // into a stack of competing cards. The control is the affordance;
201	                // the row only needs hover and a selected tint.
202	                <div className="-mx-1.5 grid">
203	                  {field.options.map((option) => (
204	                    <label
205	                      key={option.value}
206	                      className={cn(
207	                        "flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-2.5 rounded-lg px-1.5 py-1.5 text-[0.8125rem] leading-5 transition-colors",
208	                        value === option.value
209	                          ? "bg-sky-500/10 text-foreground dark:bg-sky-400/10"
210	                          : "text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted/60 hover:text-foreground",
211	                      )}
212	                    >
213	                      <input
214	                        type="radio"
215	                        name={draftKey}
216	                        value={option.value}
217	                        checked={value === option.value}
218	                        disabled={disabled}
219	                        onChange={() => workerCardManager.setElicitationDraft(draftKey, option.value)}
220	                        className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 accent-sky-600 dark:accent-sky-400"
221	                      />
222	                      <span>{option.label}</span>
223	                    </label>
224	                  ))}
225	                </div>
226	              ) : field.kind === "multi_select" ? (
227	                <div className="-mx-1.5 grid">
228	                  {field.options.map((option) => {
229	                    const optionKey = optionDraftKey(prefix, field.name, option.value);
230	                    const stored = workerCardManager.readElicitationDraft(optionKey);
231	                    const checked = stored === "true" || (stored === "" && Array.isArray(field.defaultValue) && field.defaultValue.includes(option.value));
232	                    return (
233	                      <label
234	                        key={option.value}
235	                        className={cn(
236	                          "flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-2.5 rounded-lg px-1.5 py-1.5 text-[0.8125rem] leading-5 transition-colors",
237	                          checked
238	                            ? "bg-sky-500/10 text-foreground dark:bg-sky-400/10"
239	                            : "text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted/60 hover:text-foreground",
240	                        )}
241	                      >
242	                        <input
243	                          type="checkbox"
244	                          value={option.value}
245	                          checked={checked}
246	                          disabled={disabled}
247	                          onChange={(event) => workerCardManager.setElicitationDraft(optionKey, String(event.target.checked))}
248	                          className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 rounded accent-sky-600 dark:accent-sky-400"
249	                        />
250	                        <span>{option.label}</span>
251	                      </label>
252	                    );
253	                  })}
254	                </div>
255	              ) : field.kind === "boolean" ? (
256	                <Switch
257	                  checked={value === true}
258	                  disabled={disabled}
259	                  aria-label={field.label}
260	                  onCheckedChange={(checked) => workerCardManager.setElicitationDraft(draftKey, String(checked))}
261	                />
262	              ) : field.kind === "text" ? (
263	                <Textarea
264	                  value={String(value ?? "")}
265	                  disabled={disabled}
266	                  placeholder={t("worker.elicitation.inputPlaceholder")}
267	                  onChange={(event) => workerCardManager.setElicitationDraft(draftKey, event.target.value)}
268	                  className="min-h-20 resize-y text-[0.8125rem]"
269	                />
270	              ) : (
271	                <Input
272	                  type="number"
273	                  value={String(value ?? "")}
274	                  disabled={disabled}
275	                  placeholder={t("worker.elicitation.inputPlaceholder")}
276	                  onChange={(event) => workerCardManager.setElicitationDraft(draftKey, event.target.value)}
277	                  className="text-[0.8125rem]"
278	                />
279	              )}
280	            </fieldset>
281	          );
282	        })}
283	      </div>
284	      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-1.5">
285	        <button
286	          type="button"
287	          className="inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-2.5 text-[0.8125rem] font-medium text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-muted/60 hover:text-foreground focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring disabled:opacity-50"
288	          disabled={disabled || !onRespond}
289	          onClick={() => { void submit("decline"); }}
290	        >
291	          <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
292	          {t("worker.elicitation.skip")}
293	        </button>
294	        <button
295	          type="button"
296	          className="inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-primary px-3 text-[0.8125rem] font-medium text-primary-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-primary/90 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
297	          disabled={disabled || !onRespond || missingRequired || invalid}
298	          onClick={() => { void submit("accept"); }}
299	        >
300	          <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
301	          {t(isUrl ? "worker.elicitation.done" : "worker.elicitation.submit")}
302	        </button>
303	      </div>
304	    </section>
305	  );
306	}
```

```text
11	  description: string | null;
12	  kind: "text" | "number" | "boolean" | "single_select" | "multi_select";
13	  options: ElicitationOption[];
14	  required: boolean;
15	  defaultValue?: ElicitationValue;
16	  minimum?: number;
17	  maximum?: number;
18	  minLength?: number;
19	  maxLength?: number;
20	  minItems?: number;
21	  maxItems?: number;
22	  pattern?: string;
23	};
24	
25	type UnknownRecord = Record<string, unknown>;
26	
27	function asRecord(value: unknown): UnknownRecord | null {
28	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value)
29	    ? value as UnknownRecord
30	    : null;
31	}
32	
33	function nonEmptyString(value: unknown): string | null {
34	  return typeof value === "string" && value.trim() ? value.trim() : null;
35	}
36	
37	function scalarString(value: unknown): string | null {
38	  return typeof value === "string" || typeof value === "number" || typeof value === "boolean"
39	    ? String(value)
40	    : null;
41	}
42	
43	function parseOptions(value: unknown): ElicitationOption[] {
44	  const record = asRecord(value);
45	  if (!record) return [];
46	
47	  const variants = Array.isArray(record.oneOf)
48	    ? record.oneOf
49	    : Array.isArray(record.anyOf)
50	      ? record.anyOf
51	      : [];
52	  const variantOptions = variants.flatMap((variant) => {
53	    const option = asRecord(variant);
54	    const optionValue = scalarString(option?.const);
55	    if (!option || optionValue === null) return [];
56	    return [{
57	      value: optionValue,
58	      label: nonEmptyString(option.title) ?? optionValue,
59	    }];
60	  });
61	  if (variantOptions.length > 0) return variantOptions;
62	
63	  return Array.isArray(record.enum)
64	    ? record.enum.flatMap((item) => {
65	        const optionValue = scalarString(item);
66	        return optionValue === null ? [] : [{ value: optionValue, label: optionValue }];
67	      })
68	    : [];
69	}
70	
71	function fieldKind(property: UnknownRecord, options: ElicitationOption[]): ElicitationField["kind"] {
72	  if (property.type === "array") return "multi_select";
73	  if (options.length > 0) return "single_select";
74	  if (property.type === "boolean") return "boolean";
75	  if (property.type === "number" || property.type === "integer") return "number";
76	  return "text";
77	}
78	
79	export function parseElicitationFields(schema: unknown): ElicitationField[] {
80	  const schemaRecord = asRecord(schema);
81	  const properties = asRecord(schemaRecord?.properties) ?? {};
82	  const required = new Set(
83	    Array.isArray(schemaRecord?.required)
84	      ? schemaRecord.required.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string")
85	      : [],
86	  );
87	
88	  return Object.entries(properties).flatMap(([name, rawProperty]) => {
89	    const property = asRecord(rawProperty);
90	    if (!property) return [];
91	    const options = property.type === "array"
92	      ? parseOptions(property.items)
93	      : parseOptions(property);
94	    return [{
95	      name,
96	      label: nonEmptyString(property.title) ?? (name.replace(/^question_/, "").replace(/[_-]+/g, " ").trim() || name),
97	      description: nonEmptyString(property.description),
98	      kind: fieldKind(property, options),
99	      options,
100	      required: required.has(name),
101	      ...(typeof property.default === "string" || typeof property.default === "number" || typeof property.default === "boolean" || (Array.isArray(property.default) && property.default.every((item) => typeof item === "string"))
102	        ? { defaultValue: property.default as ElicitationValue }
103	        : {}),
104	      ...(typeof property.minimum === "number" ? { minimum: property.minimum } : {}),
105	      ...(typeof property.maximum === "number" ? { maximum: property.maximum } : {}),
106	      ...(typeof property.minLength === "number" ? { minLength: property.minLength } : {}),
107	      ...(typeof property.maxLength === "number" ? { maxLength: property.maxLength } : {}),
108	      ...(typeof property.minItems === "number" ? { minItems: property.minItems } : {}),
109	      ...(typeof property.maxItems === "number" ? { maxItems: property.maxItems } : {}),
110	      ...(typeof property.pattern === "string" ? { pattern: property.pattern } : {}),
111	    }];
112	  });
113	}
114	
115	export function buildElicitationContent(
116	  fields: readonly ElicitationField[],
117	  values: Readonly<Record<string, ElicitationValue | undefined>>,
118	): Record<string, ElicitationValue> {
119	  return Object.fromEntries(fields.flatMap((field) => {
120	    const value = values[field.name];
121	    if (value === undefined) return [];
122	    if (field.kind === "number" && typeof value === "string") {
123	      const numberValue = Number(value);
124	      return Number.isFinite(numberValue) ? [[field.name, numberValue] as const] : [];
125	    }
126	    return [[field.name, value] as const];
127	  }));
128	}
129	
130	export function hasMissingRequiredElicitationField(
131	  fields: readonly ElicitationField[],
132	  values: Readonly<Record<string, ElicitationValue | undefined>>,
133	) {
134	  return fields.some((field) => {
135	    if (!field.required) return false;
136	    const value = values[field.name];
137	    return Array.isArray(value) ? value.length === 0 : value === undefined || value === "";
138	  });
139	}
140	
141	export function hasInvalidElicitationField(
142	  fields: readonly ElicitationField[],
143	  values: Readonly<Record<string, ElicitationValue | undefined>>,
144	) {
145	  return fields.some((field) => {
146	    const value = values[field.name];
147	    if (value === undefined || value === "" || (Array.isArray(value) && value.length === 0)) return false;
148	    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
149	      return (field.minItems !== undefined && value.length < field.minItems)
150	        || (field.maxItems !== undefined && value.length > field.maxItems);
151	    }
152	    if (field.kind === "number") {
153	      const numeric = typeof value === "number" ? value : Number(value);
154	      return !Number.isFinite(numeric)
155	        || (field.minimum !== undefined && numeric < field.minimum)
156	        || (field.maximum !== undefined && numeric > field.maximum);
157	    }
158	    if (typeof value === "string") {
159	      if (field.minLength !== undefined && value.length < field.minLength) return true;
160	      if (field.maxLength !== undefined && value.length > field.maxLength) return true;
161	      if (field.pattern) {
162	        try {
163	          return !new RegExp(field.pattern).test(value);
164	        } catch {
165	          return true;
166	        }
167	      }
168	    }
169	    return false;
170	  });
171	}
172
```

```text
1	import type { WorkerEntry } from "@/shared/worker-entries";
2	
3	export type PendingWorkerElicitation = {
4	  requestId: number;
5	  requestedAt: string;
6	  sessionId?: string | null;
7	  toolCallId?: string | null;
8	  mode?: "form" | "url" | null;
9	  elicitationId?: string | null;
10	  url?: string | null;
11	  message?: string | null;
12	  requestedSchema?: {
13	    type?: string;
14	    properties?: Record<string, unknown>;
15	    required?: string[];
16	  } | null;
17	};
18	
19	const TERMINAL_ELICITATION_STATUSES = new Set([
20	  "answered",
21	  "cancelled",
22	  "canceled",
23	  "completed",
24	  "declined",
25	  "failed",
26	  "rejected",
27	  "skipped",
28	]);
29	
30	function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
31	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value)
32	    ? value as Record<string, unknown>
33	    : null;
34	}
35	
36	function asString(value: unknown): string | null {
37	  return typeof value === "string" ? value : null;
38	}
39	
40	function asRequestedSchema(value: unknown): PendingWorkerElicitation["requestedSchema"] {
41	  const schema = asRecord(value);
42	  if (!schema) {
43	    return null;
44	  }
45	  const properties = asRecord(schema.properties) ?? {};
46	  const required = Array.isArray(schema.required)
47	    ? schema.required.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string")
48	    : undefined;
49	  const type = asString(schema.type);
50	  return {
51	    properties,
52	    ...(type ? { type } : {}),
53	    ...(required ? { required } : {}),
54	  };
55	}
56	
57	function normalizeElicitationStatus(entry: WorkerEntry) {
58	  return (entry.status ?? "pending").trim().toLowerCase();
59	}
60	
61	function isOpenElicitationStatus(entry: WorkerEntry) {
62	  return !TERMINAL_ELICITATION_STATUSES.has(normalizeElicitationStatus(entry));
63	}
64	
65	function entryRequestId(entry: WorkerEntry) {
66	  const raw = asRecord(entry.raw);
67	  const requestId = raw?.requestId;
68	  return typeof requestId === "number" && Number.isFinite(requestId) ? requestId : null;
69	}
70	
71	function pendingFromEntry(entry: WorkerEntry): PendingWorkerElicitation | null {
72	  const raw = asRecord(entry.raw);
73	  const requestId = entryRequestId(entry);
74	  if (!raw || requestId === null || !isOpenElicitationStatus(entry)) {
75	    return null;
76	  }
77	  return {
78	    requestId,
79	    requestedAt: entry.timestamp,
80	    sessionId: asString(raw.sessionId),
81	    toolCallId: asString(raw.toolCallId ?? entry.toolCallId),
82	    mode: raw.mode === "form" || raw.mode === "url" ? raw.mode : null,
83	    elicitationId: asString(raw.elicitationId),
84	    url: asString(raw.url),
85	    message: asString(raw.message),
86	    requestedSchema: asRequestedSchema(raw.requestedSchema),
87	  };
88	}
89	
90	export function derivePendingElicitationsFromWorkerEntries(entries: readonly WorkerEntry[]): PendingWorkerElicitation[] {
91	  const pendingByRequestId = new Map<number, PendingWorkerElicitation>();
92	
93	  for (const entry of entries) {
94	    if (entry.type !== "elicitation") {
95	      continue;
96	    }
97	    const requestId = entryRequestId(entry);
98	    if (requestId === null) {
99	      continue;
100	    }
101	    if (!isOpenElicitationStatus(entry)) {
102	      pendingByRequestId.delete(requestId);
103	      continue;
104	    }
105	    const pending = pendingFromEntry(entry);
106	    if (pending) {
107	      pendingByRequestId.set(requestId, pending);
108	    }
109	  }
110	
111	  return Array.from(pendingByRequestId.values()).sort((left, right) => {
112	    const timeDelta = new Date(left.requestedAt).getTime() - new Date(right.requestedAt).getTime();
113	    return timeDelta || left.requestId - right.requestId;
114	  });
115	}
116
```

```text
53	export type PairingState<TPairing, TStatus> = {
54	  pairing: TPairing | null;
55	  pairingStatus: TStatus | null;
56	  qrDataUrl: string | null;
57	  isLoading: boolean;
58	  isActivating: boolean;
59	  error: string | null;
60	  copyNotice: string | null;
61	  nowMs: number;
62	};
63	
64	function pairingIdentifier(pairing: unknown) {
65	  if (!pairing || typeof pairing !== "object") {
66	    return null;
67	  }
68	  const candidate = pairing as { pairingId?: unknown; id?: unknown };
69	  return typeof candidate.pairingId === "string"
70	    ? candidate.pairingId
71	    : typeof candidate.id === "string"
72	      ? candidate.id
73	      : null;
74	}
75	
76	export class PairDeviceStateManager extends StateManager<PairingState<unknown, string>> {
77	  private statusPollSeq = 0;
78	  private activeStatusPoll: { pairingId: string; requestId: number } | null = null;
79	
80	  constructor() {
81	    super({
82	      pairing: null,
83	      pairingStatus: null,
84	      qrDataUrl: null,
85	      isLoading: false,
86	      isActivating: false,
87	      error: null,
88	      copyNotice: null,
89	      nowMs: Date.now(),
90	    });
91	  }
92	
93	  patchIfCurrentPairing = (
94	    pairingId: string,
95	    patch: Partial<PairingState<unknown, string>> | ((current: PairingState<unknown, string>) => Partial<PairingState<unknown, string>>),
96	  ) => this.patch((current) => (
97	    pairingIdentifier(current.pairing) === pairingId
98	      ? typeof patch === "function"
99	        ? patch(current)
100	        : patch
101	      : {}
102	  ));
103	
104	  beginStatusPoll = (pairingId: string) => {
105	    const requestId = ++this.statusPollSeq;
106	    this.activeStatusPoll = pairingIdentifier(this.getSnapshot().pairing) === pairingId
107	      ? { pairingId, requestId }
108	      : null;
109	    return requestId;
110	  };
111	
112	  patchIfCurrentStatusPoll = (
113	    pairingId: string,
114	    requestId: number,
115	    patch: Partial<PairingState<unknown, string>> | ((current: PairingState<unknown, string>) => Partial<PairingState<unknown, string>>),
116	  ) => this.patch((current) => (
117	    pairingIdentifier(current.pairing) === pairingId
118	      && this.activeStatusPoll?.pairingId === pairingId
119	      && this.activeStatusPoll.requestId === requestId
120	      ? typeof patch === "function"
121	        ? patch(current)
122	        : patch
123	      : {}
124	  ));
125	
126	  reset = () => this.patch({
127	    pairing: null,
128	    pairingStatus: null,
129	    qrDataUrl: null,
130	    isActivating: false,
131	    error: null,
132	    copyNotice: null,
133	  });
134	}

142

146	const FILE_VIEWER_RENDER_MARKDOWN_STORAGE_KEY = "omni-file-viewer-render-markdown";
147	
148	export class FileViewerPanelManager extends StateManager<{ wordWrap: boolean; renderMarkdown: boolean }> {
149	  constructor() {
150	    super({ wordWrap: true, renderMarkdown: true });
151	  }
152	
153	  hydrateFromLocalStorage() {
154	    if (typeof window === "undefined" || typeof window.localStorage === "undefined") {
155	      return;
156	    }
157	    const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(FILE_VIEWER_RENDER_MARKDOWN_STORAGE_KEY);
158	    if (stored === "true" || stored === "false") {
159	      this.setKey("renderMarkdown", stored === "true");

161	  }
162

165	  toggleWordWrap = () => this.setKey("wordWrap", (current) => !current);
166	
167	  toggleRenderMarkdown = () => {

171	        window.localStorage.setItem(FILE_VIEWER_RENDER_MARKDOWN_STORAGE_KEY, String(next));
```

```text
28	function requestDraftPrefix(workerId: string, elicitation: PendingWorkerElicitation) {
29	  return [
30	    workerId,
31	    elicitation.sessionId ?? "session",
32	    elicitation.requestId,
33	    elicitation.toolCallId ?? "tool",
34	    "",
35	  ].join(":");
36	}
37	
38	function optionDraftKey(prefix: string, fieldName: string, optionValue: string) {
39	  return `${prefix}${fieldName}:option:${optionValue}`;
40	}
41	
42	export function InlineElicitation({
43	  workerId,
44	  elicitation,
45	  onRespond,
```

## Claude subagent agent-a6b0aeaf641beebec: Claude · 2026-08-05T10:15:55.297Z

````text
Perfect! Now I have all the information. Let me create a comprehensive summary with all the details requested.

## Summary

I've thoroughly analyzed the AskUserQuestion UI code in the omniharness repository. Here are my findings:

---

### 1. **Files & Components Rendering the Question UI**

**Primary component:**
- **`/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/agent-interactions/InlineElicitation.tsx`** (lines 42-306)
  - Main React component that renders the entire elicitation form UI
  - Uses tabs/sections for multi-question scenarios
  - Handles all field types: single_select, multi_select, text, number, boolean

**Schema parsing:**
- **`/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/lib/acp/elicitation-schema.ts`** (lines 79-128)
  - `parseElicitationFields()` - parses the schema into fields
  - `buildElicitationContent()` - assembles final answers for submission
  - Type definitions: `ElicitationField`, `ElicitationValue`, `ElicitationOption`

**State management:**
- **`/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/component-state-managers.ts`** (lines 250-331)
  - `workerCardManager` StateManager class
  - Methods: `setElicitationDraft()`, `readElicitationDraft()`, `clearElicitationDrafts()`

**Data model:**
- **`/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/worker-elicitations.ts`** (lines 1-88)
  - `PendingWorkerElicitation` type definition

---

### 2. **Tabs/Sections Modeling in State**

The tabs are based on **question field naming convention**: fields matching `/^question_\d+$/` (e.g., `question_0`, `question_1`, `question_2`) become tabs.

**Key lines in InlineElicitation.tsx:**

```typescript
// Lines 57-59: Detect question fields and whether to use tabs
const fields = parseElicitationFields(elicitation.requestedSchema);
const questionFields = fields.filter((field) => /^question_\d+$/.test(field.name));
const usesQuestionTabs = questionFields.length > 1;
```

**Tab state management (lines 63-67):**
```typescript
const prefix = requestDraftPrefix(workerId, elicitation);
const activeQuestionDraftKey = `${prefix}active-question`;
const storedActiveQuestion = workerCardManager.readElicitationDraft(activeQuestionDraftKey);
const activeQuestion = questionFields.find((field) => field.name === storedActiveQuestion)
  ?? questionFields[0]
  ?? null;
```

The **active tab** is stored using a single key: `${prefix}active-question`

**Tab visibility (lines 68-70):**
```typescript
const visibleFields = usesQuestionTabs
  ? fields.filter((field) => !/^question_\d+$/.test(field.name) || field.name === activeQuestion?.name)
  : fields;
```

This filters to show only:
- Non-question fields (e.g., `customAnswer` - the "Other" field)
- The currently active question tab

---

### 3. **"Other" Option Storage: NOT Per-Question, But GLOBAL/SHARED**

**Critical Finding:** The "Other" option is stored as a **single shared field named `customAnswer`** across all questions, NOT keyed per question index.

**Schema definition pattern (from test file `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/tests/app/acp/elicitation-schema.test.ts`, lines 8-29):**

```typescript
const INCIDENT_SCHEMA = {
  type: "object",
  properties: {
    question_0: {
      type: "array",
      title: "Niche access",
      items: {
        anyOf: [
          { const: "Agencies / freelancer world", title: "Agencies / freelancer world — You know agency owners." },
          { const: "Legal / professional services", title: "Legal / professional services — Lawyers or accountants." },
          { const: "Property / recruiting", title: "Property / recruiting — Contacts in either market." },
          { const: "No special access", title: "No special access — Pick on market logic." },
        ],
      },
    },
    customAnswer: {
      type: "string",
      title: "Other",
      description: "Type your own answer instead of choosing an option above (optional).",
    },
  },
};
```

**State declaration (component-state-managers.ts, lines 250-264):**

```typescript
export const workerCardManager = new class extends StateManager<{
  openByWorkerId: Record<string, boolean>;
  permissionOpenByWorkerId: Record<string, boolean>;
  elicitationOpenByWorkerId: Record<string, boolean>;
  elicitationDraftsByKey: Record<string, string>;  // <-- all drafts keyed by string
  terminalProcessesOpenByWorkerId: Record<string, boolean>;
}> {
```

**How "Other" is stored (InlineElicitation.tsx, lines 86-87):**

```typescript
const stored = workerCardManager.readElicitationDraft(`${prefix}${field.name}`);
return [field.name, stored !== "" ? stored : field.defaultValue ?? ""];
```

For the `customAnswer` field:
- Storage key: `${prefix}customAnswer` (where prefix includes workerId, sessionId, requestId, toolCallId)
- Type: "text" field (line 262 of InlineElicitation: `field.kind === "text"`)
- **Rendered as a `<Textarea>` (line 263-269)**
- Single instance: NOT replicated per question tab

**Example storage key structure (lines 28-35):**
```typescript
function requestDraftPrefix(workerId: string, elicitation: PendingWorkerElicitation) {
  return [
    workerId,
    elicitation.sessionId ?? "session",
    elicitation.requestId,
    elicitation.toolCallId ?? "tool",
    "",
  ].join(":");
}
// Example: "my-worker:session-123:1:tool-xyz:"
```

**Multi-select option storage (lines 38-39):**
```typescript
function optionDraftKey(prefix: string, fieldName: string, optionValue: string) {
  return `${prefix}${fieldName}:option:${optionValue}`;
}
```

This shows that **individual checkbox states** within a multi_select are keyed per field and per option value, BUT the `customAnswer` field itself is global (not per-question).

---

### 4. **Final Answer Assembly & Submission**

**Submission flow (lines 92-105):**

```typescript
const submit = async (action: ElicitationResponseInput["action"]) => {
  try {
    await onRespond?.({
      workerId,
      requestId: elicitation.requestId,
      action,
      ...(action === "accept" ? { content: buildElicitationContent(fields, values) } : {}),
    });
    workerCardManager.clearElicitationDrafts(prefix);
  } catch {
    // The mutation owner restores the pending interaction and surfaces the
    // error; retaining the request-token draft lets the user retry safely.
  }
};
```

**Content building (elicitation-schema.ts, lines 115-128):**

```typescript
export function buildElicitationContent(
  fields: readonly ElicitationField[],
  values: Readonly<Record<string, ElicitationValue | undefined>>,
): Record<string, ElicitationValue> {
  return Object.fromEntries(fields.flatMap((field) => {
    const value = values[field.name];
    if (value === undefined) return [];
    if (field.kind === "number" && typeof value === "string") {
      const numberValue = Number(value);
      return Number.isFinite(numberValue) ? [[field.name, numberValue] as const] : [];
    }
    return [[field.name, value] as const];
  }));
}
```

**Example response payload (from test `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/tests/app/acp/elicitation-schema.test.ts`, lines 55-64):**

```typescript
buildElicitationContent(fields, {
  question_0: ["Agencies / freelancer world", "Property / recruiting"],
  customAnswer: "I know two founders",
})).toEqual({
  question_0: ["Agencies / freelancer world", "Property / recruiting"],
  customAnswer: "I know two founders",
});
```

This demonstrates that:
- Each question field (e.g., `question_0`) maintains its array of selected options
- The `customAnswer` field is transmitted as a separate top-level property (not nested)
- All answers are combined into a flat object keyed by field name

**ElicitationResponseInput type (lines 21-26):**

```typescript
export type ElicitationResponseInput = {
  workerId: string;
  requestId: number;
  action: "accept" | "decline" | "cancel";
  content?: Record<string, ElicitationValue>;
};
```

---

### Key Insights

1. **No per-question "Other" state**: The `customAnswer` field is **global to the entire form**, not replicated for each question tab.
2. **Session storage**: All draft values are persisted to `window.sessionStorage` with keys like `omniharness:elicitation:${key}` (component-state-managers.ts, lines 292-308).
3. **Tab switching**: Only the active question is visible at a time, but all field values (including `customAnswer`) are maintained in memory across tab switches.
4. **Clean submission**: Upon successful submit, `clearElicitationDrafts(prefix)` removes all prefixed keys from both state manager and sessionStorage.

parent: 62df97c0-c7cb-4cf9-80c4-0e878b4f1622
````

```text
28	function requestDraftPrefix(workerId: string, elicitation: PendingWorkerElicitation) {
29	  return [
30	    workerId,
31	    elicitation.sessionId ?? "session",
32	    elicitation.requestId,
33	    elicitation.toolCallId ?? "tool",
34	    "",
35	  ].join(":");
36	}
37	
38	function optionDraftKey(prefix: string, fieldName: string, optionValue: string) {
39	  return `${prefix}${fieldName}:option:${optionValue}`;
40	}
41	
42	export function InlineElicitation({
43	  workerId,
44	  elicitation,
45	  onRespond,
46	  disabled = false,
47	  className,
48	}: {
49	  workerId: string;
50	  elicitation: PendingWorkerElicitation;
51	  onRespond?: (input: ElicitationResponseInput) => void | Promise<unknown>;
52	  disabled?: boolean;
53	  className?: string;
54	}) {
55	  useI18nSnapshot();
56	  useManagerSnapshot(workerCardManager);
57	  const fields = parseElicitationFields(elicitation.requestedSchema);
58	  const questionFields = fields.filter((field) => /^question_\d+$/.test(field.name));
59	  const usesQuestionTabs = questionFields.length > 1;
60	  const isUrl = elicitation.mode === "url" && Boolean(elicitation.url);
61	  const safeUrl = isUrl && /^https?:\/\//i.test(elicitation.url ?? "") ? elicitation.url : null;
62	  const prefix = requestDraftPrefix(workerId, elicitation);
63	  const activeQuestionDraftKey = `${prefix}active-question`;
64	  const storedActiveQuestion = workerCardManager.readElicitationDraft(activeQuestionDraftKey);
65	  const activeQuestion = questionFields.find((field) => field.name === storedActiveQuestion)
66	    ?? questionFields[0]
67	    ?? null;
68	  const visibleFields = usesQuestionTabs
69	    ? fields.filter((field) => !/^question_\d+$/.test(field.name) || field.name === activeQuestion?.name)
70	    : fields;
71	  const values = Object.fromEntries(fields.map((field) => {
72	    if (field.kind === "multi_select") {
73	      const defaults = Array.isArray(field.defaultValue) ? field.defaultValue : [];
74	      const selected = field.options

77	          return stored === "true" || (stored === "" && defaults.includes(option.value));
```

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-05T10:16:43.442Z

The `customAnswer` field in the schema comes from Claude Code's ACP adapter upstream, so the omniharness receives a single string field for the entire AskUserQuestion request regardless of how many questions there are. I should check if the agent process is an external npm package by looking at the bridge configuration and dependencies to understand the real schema structure.

The fix needs to happen client-side in omniharness — I'd need to synthesize a per-question "Other" input and fold the answers back together. But the challenge is how the response gets sent back so the agent understands it, since the ACP response is a flat object matching the schema, and upstream might reject or ignore extra keys if I try to send `customAnswer` per question. Let me look for the actual ACP adapter package to see how upstream handles the response.

```text
fi

if [ -z "$DOCKER_BIN" ]; then
  if command -v docker >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    DOCKER_BIN="docker"
  elif command -v podman >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    DOCKER_BIN="podman"
```

```text
    return {

}
/**
 * Fold an ACP elicitation response into the AskUserQuestion tool's input.
 *
 * Selected labels are read back from the indexed form fields and written into
 * `answers` as a `{ [questionText]: label }` map (comma-joining multi-selects)
 * — the key shape the tool's own `call()` reads. Free text from the custom-
 * answer field becomes the tool's top-level `response`. Decline yields empty
 * answers (the model is told the user skipped rather than the turn aborting);
 * cancel aborts the tool call.
 */
export function applyAskElicitationResponse(response, toolInput, questions) {
    if (response.action === "cancel") {
        return { action: "cancel" };
    }
    if (response.action === "decline") {
        return { action: "answered", updatedInput: { ...toolInput, answers: {} } };
    }
    const content = response.content ?? {};
    // Typed against the tool's own output schema so the answer/response shapes
    // stay in sync with what the built-in tool's call() expects to read back.
    const answers = {};
    questions.forEach((question, index) => {
        const value = content[questionFieldKey(index)];
        if (value === undefined || value === null) {
            return;
        }
        const text = Array.isArray(value) ? value.join(", ") : String(value);
        if (text === "") {
            return;
        }
        answers[question.question] = text;
    });
    const updatedInput = { ...toolInput, answers };
    const custom = content[CUSTOM_ANSWER_FIELD];
    if (typeof custom === "string" && custom.trim() !== "") {
        const response = custom;
        updatedInput.response = response;
    }
    return { action: "answered", updatedInput };
}
/**
 * Coerce an arbitrary MCP `requestedSchema` into an ACP `ElicitationSchema`.
 * The two are structurally compatible JSON Schemas; we just guarantee the
 * `type: "object"` discriminator is present.
 */
function normalizeElicitationSchema(schema) {
    if (!schema || typeof schema !== "object") {
        return { type: "object", properties: {} };
    }
    return { ...schema, type: "object" };
}
```

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-05T10:17:35.072Z

I'm tracing back to the root cause now — the issue originates upstream in the `@agentclientprotocol/claude-agent-acp` adapter, not in omniharness's UI layer. The adapter consolidates all responses into a single `customAnswer` field that gets folded into `updatedInput.response` as one top-level string, which means there's no per-question channel for free text responses in the wire protocol itself. The user's observation about this limitation checks out.

Looking at how Claude Code's own AskUserQuestion UI handles this, each question tab has its own "Other" option, and the answer for that specific question gets mapped into the `answers` object keyed by the question text. So a free-text "Other" response for question N should land in `answers[question_N.question]`, not in some global response field.

I can see two paths forward. Option A is UI-only on our side — render a per-question "Other" text input inside each tab and fold it into that question's own field value before submitting, sending `question_N: "<typed text>"` instead of `customAnswer`. This works without any upstream changes because `applyAskElicitationResponse` just does string conversion and joining on whatever value is in `question_N` and puts it in the right answer slot without validating against the enum, so typed text flows through correctly. The caveat is that the schema definition...

Actually, I'm realizing the real issue is what to do with the `customAnswer` field that upstream still declares. There's a semantic distinction here — `customAnswer` maps to `updatedInput.response`, which is the "user typed their own answer instead of the whole question set" channel. In the single-question case that's exactly right, but in the multi-question case it's ambiguous. So my recommendation is to keep the current behavior for single questions, but for multiple questions with tabs, render an "Other" input inside each tab keyed per question and fold it into `question_N`, while dropping or hiding the global `customAnswer` field.

The code changes needed are in `InlineElicitation.tsx` — `visibleFields` currently leaks `customAnswer` into every tab because it's a non-question field, which is exactly the bug the user sees. Draft keys are already per-field-name and prefix-scoped, so a per-question other just needs a new key like `${prefix}${field.name}:other`. Then `buildElicitationContent` in `elicitation-schema.ts` would need to merge the per-question other text into `question_N`. I also need to consider the rendering — currently `customAnswer` renders as a textarea below the option list, but based on the user's description, it should actually appear as an option in the list itself, and selecting it opens a text input.

The existing test in `inline-agent-interactions.test.tsx` that checks for "Other" would still pass, but it would need updating to assert per-tab behavior. Before I proceed with the fix, I should clarify whether the user wants me to implement this or just present the analysis.

There's also a design decision to surface: should each tab have its own "Other" text input always visible, or should "Other" be a radio/checkbox option that reveals a text box when selected? And should we keep a global notes field alongside per-question ones?

Let me verify the exact behavior at line 69 where `visibleFields` filters to show non-question fields in every tab — confirming that `customAnswer` renders as a shared textarea across all tabs, which matches what the user observed.

## Claude · 2026-08-05T10:17:45.921Z

You're right, and I found the exact mechanism. Here's the diagnosis — no changes made.

## What's happening

`src/components/agent-interactions/InlineElicitation.tsx:68-70`:

```ts
const visibleFields = usesQuestionTabs
  ? fields.filter((field) => !/^question_\d+$/.test(field.name) || field.name === activeQuestion?.name)
  : fields;
```

Tabs filter *question* fields down to the active one, but any **non**-question field passes through unconditionally. `customAnswer` is a non-question field, so it renders in every tab — and since its draft key is a single `${prefix}customAnswer` (line 86), it's literally one value shared across all tabs. Type in tab 1, it's there in tab 2. That's what you're seeing.

## But the root cause is upstream, not in this repo

The schema comes from `@agentclientprotocol/claude-agent-acp`. In `dist/elicitation.js:84-102`, it emits `question_0..N` inside a loop, then appends **one** `customAnswer` field outside the loop:

```js
questions.forEach((question, index) => { properties[questionFieldKey(index)] = ... });
properties[CUSTOM_ANSWER_FIELD] = { type: "string", title: "Other", ... };
```

And on the way back (`applyAskElicitationResponse`), `customAnswer` becomes `updatedInput.response` — a single top-level string — while each `question_N` becomes `answers[questionText]`. So the wire protocol as the adapter defines it has **no per-question free-text channel at all**. The adapter models "Other" as one global "instead of answering, here's what I actually want", which is coherent for a single question and wrong for several.

## The fix we can make locally

The adapter does **no enum validation** on the response — it just does `Array.isArray(value) ? value.join(", ") : String(value)` and drops it into `answers[question.question]`. So free text written into `question_N` lands as that question's answer correctly. That means omniharness can fix this client-side without touching upstream:

1. When `usesQuestionTabs`, exclude `customAnswer` from `visibleFields` and render a per-question "Other" input inside each tab, keyed `${prefix}${field.name}:other`.
2. Before submit, fold each tab's other-text into its own `question_N` value (replace for single-select, append to the array for multi-select) rather than sending `customAnswer`.
3. Leave the single-question path exactly as-is — one "Other" box mapping to `customAnswer` is correct there, and it's what feeds the tool's top-level `response`.

Touches `InlineElicitation.tsx` plus probably `buildElicitationContent` in `src/lib/acp/elicitation-schema.ts`; `tests/ui/inline-agent-interactions.test.tsx:46-92` would need per-tab assertions (its current `toContain("Other")` check passes either way, which is why this slipped through).

One design call before I'd touch anything:

uuid: 7c93cb73-d530-496f-92b7-b5fb654f5d5f
parent: 733151de-525e-48ad-81c7-46cd77ce2367

```text
45	  if (!record) return [];
46	
47	  const variants = Array.isArray(record.oneOf)
48	    ? record.oneOf
49	    : Array.isArray(record.anyOf)
50	      ? record.anyOf
51	      : [];
52	  const variantOptions = variants.flatMap((variant) => {
53	    const option = asRecord(variant);
54	    const optionValue = scalarString(option?.const);
55	    if (!option || optionValue === null) return [];
56	    return [{
57	      value: optionValue,
58	      label: nonEmptyString(option.title) ?? optionValue,
59	    }];
60	  });
61	  if (variantOptions.length > 0) return variantOptions;
62	
63	  return Array.isArray(record.enum)
64	    ? record.enum.flatMap((item) => {
65	        const optionValue = scalarString(item);
66	        return optionValue === null ? [] : [{ value: optionValue, label: optionValue }];
67	      })
68	    : [];
69	}
70	
71	function fieldKind(property: UnknownRecord, options: ElicitationOption[]): ElicitationField["kind"] {
72	  if (property.type === "array") return "multi_select";
73	  if (options.length > 0) return "single_select";
74	  if (property.type === "boolean") return "boolean";
75	  if (property.type === "number" || property.type === "integer") return "number";
76	  return "text";
77	}
78	
79	export function parseElicitationFields(schema: unknown): ElicitationField[] {
80	  const schemaRecord = asRecord(schema);
81	  const properties = asRecord(schemaRecord?.properties) ?? {};
82	  const required = new Set(
83	    Array.isArray(schemaRecord?.required)
84	      ? schemaRecord.required.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string")
85	      : [],
86	  );
87	
88	  return Object.entries(properties).flatMap(([name, rawProperty]) => {
89	    const property = asRecord(rawProperty);
90	    if (!property) return [];
91	    const options = property.type === "array"
92	      ? parseOptions(property.items)
93	      : parseOptions(property);
94	    return [{
95	      name,
96	      label: nonEmptyString(property.title) ?? (name.replace(/^question_/, "").replace(/[_-]+/g, " ").trim() || name),
97	      description: nonEmptyString(property.description),
98	      kind: fieldKind(property, options),
99	      options,
100	      required: required.has(name),
101	      ...(typeof property.default === "string" || typeof property.default === "number" || typeof property.default === "boolean" || (Array.isArray(property.default) && property.default.every((item) => typeof item === "string"))
102	        ? { defaultValue: property.default as ElicitationValue }
103	        : {}),
104	      ...(typeof property.minimum === "number" ? { minimum: property.minimum } : {}),
105	      ...(typeof property.maximum === "number" ? { maximum: property.maximum } : {}),
106	      ...(typeof property.minLength === "number" ? { minLength: property.minLength } : {}),
107	      ...(typeof property.maxLength === "number" ? { maxLength: property.maxLength } : {}),
108	      ...(typeof property.minItems === "number" ? { minItems: property.minItems } : {}),
109	      ...(typeof property.maxItems === "number" ? { maxItems: property.maxItems } : {}),
110	      ...(typeof property.pattern === "string" ? { pattern: property.pattern } : {}),
111	    }];
112	  });
113	}
114	
115	export function buildElicitationContent(
116	  fields: readonly ElicitationField[],
117	  values: Readonly<Record<string, ElicitationValue | undefined>>,
118	): Record<string, ElicitationValue> {
119	  return Object.fromEntries(fields.flatMap((field) => {
120	    const value = values[field.name];
121	    if (value === undefined) return [];
122	    if (field.kind === "number" && typeof value === "string") {
123	      const numberValue = Number(value);
124	      return Number.isFinite(numberValue) ? [[field.name, numberValue] as const] : [];
125	    }
126	    return [[field.name, value] as const];
127	  }));
128	}
129	
130	export function hasMissingRequiredElicitationField(
131	  fields: readonly ElicitationField[],
132	  values: Readonly<Record<string, ElicitationValue | undefined>>,
133	) {
134	  return fields.some((field) => {
135	    if (!field.required) return false;
136	    const value = values[field.name];
137	    return Array.isArray(value) ? value.length === 0 : value === undefined || value === "";
138	  });
139	}
140	
141	export function hasInvalidElicitationField(
142	  fields: readonly ElicitationField[],
143	  values: Readonly<Record<string, ElicitationValue | undefined>>,
144	) {
145	  return fields.some((field) => {
146	    const value = values[field.name];
147	    if (value === undefined || value === "" || (Array.isArray(value) && value.length === 0)) return false;
148	    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
149	      return (field.minItems !== undefined && value.length < field.minItems)
150	        || (field.maxItems !== undefined && value.length > field.maxItems);
151	    }
152	    if (field.kind === "number") {
153	      const numeric = typeof value === "number" ? value : Number(value);
154	      return !Number.isFinite(numeric)
155	        || (field.minimum !== undefined && numeric < field.minimum)
156	        || (field.maximum !== undefined && numeric > field.maximum);
157	    }
158	    if (typeof value === "string") {
159	      if (field.minLength !== undefined && value.length < field.minLength) return true;
160	      if (field.maxLength !== undefined && value.length > field.maxLength) return true;
161	      if (field.pattern) {
162	        try {
163	          return !new RegExp(field.pattern).test(value);
164	        } catch {
165	          return true;
166	        }
167	      }
168	    }
169	    return false;
170	  });
171	}
172
```

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-05T10:22:49.659Z

I'm realizing I can reuse the existing title and description from the `customAnswer` field in the schema rather than creating new i18n keys, which keeps things simpler and avoids duplicating the "Other" label and placeholder text.

For the design, I'll find the `customAnswer` field when rendering tabbed questions, exclude it from the visible fields, and render a per-question textarea using the field's own label and description. If there's no `customAnswer` field in the schema, I'll just skip the per-question Other option entirely.

For merging responses, I need to handle the case where a user types text in the Other field — if they've entered something, that typed text should take precedence over any radio selection, since the intent is to let them provide their own answer instead of choosing a preset option.

Now I'm thinking through the validation and state management. If I fold the other-text into the values object at construction time rather than waiting until submission, then validation checks and the visual feedback all stay in sync. But that would make the radio button appear deselected when the user types, which could feel jarring as they're typing. I need to balance between keeping the state consistent and not creating confusing visual feedback.

For multi-select, folding the typed text into the values array works cleanly since the checkboxes read from a different source. For single-select, the radio uses the value directly, so typing would visually deselect it — which is actually honest feedback that the typed text has taken over. I think that's the right approach.

The implementation plan is to compute the folded values in InlineElicitation and filter out the custom answer field when building the content. Building the values object from answerFields, where each field's value is computed based on its kind—for multi-select fields, free text answers are appended to the selected options, and for other field types the stored value is retrieved. Refining the guard condition...

I should be more precise and only apply the Other box to select-kind question fields. I'm creating a helper function that checks if a field is a question field with either single or multi-select options, then using that to gate the `perQuestionOther` logic. This way the Other box only appears where it makes sense—alongside actual option sets. The rest of the flow stays the same: `buildElicitationContent` handles submission and validation uses the answer fields.

For rendering, I'm adding the Other textarea conditionally after the options. I need to pull the raw stored text directly from the draft manager rather than using a trimmed version, so the textarea displays exactly what was saved.

The drafts get cleared on submit using a prefix-based approach, which should handle the `:other` keys automatically. Let me check the component state manager to confirm the clearElicitationDrafts function is properly scoped to the prefix.

```text
297	      ...current,
298	      [key]: value,
299	    }));
300	  };
301	
302	  readElicitationDraft = (key: string) => {
303	    const current = this.getSnapshot().elicitationDraftsByKey[key];
304	    if (current !== undefined) return current;
305	    return typeof window === "undefined"
306	      ? ""
307	      : window.sessionStorage.getItem(`omniharness:elicitation:${key}`) ?? "";
308	  };
309	
310	  clearElicitationDrafts = (prefix: string) => this.setKey("elicitationDraftsByKey", (current) => {
311	    const next = { ...current };
312	    for (const key of Object.keys(next)) {
313	      if (key.startsWith(prefix)) {
314	        delete next[key];
315	      }
316	    }
317	    if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
318	      const storagePrefix = `omniharness:elicitation:${prefix}`;
319	      for (let index = window.sessionStorage.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
320	        const key = window.sessionStorage.key(index);
321	        if (key?.startsWith(storagePrefix)) window.sessionStorage.removeItem(key);
322	      }
323	    }
324	    return next;
325	  });
326	
327	  setTerminalProcessesOpen = (workerId: string, open: boolean) => this.setKey("terminalProcessesOpenByWorkerId", (current) => ({
328	    ...current,
329	    [workerId]: open,
330	  }));
331	}();
332
```

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-05T10:23:07.317Z

Now I'm implementing the changes in InlineElicitation.tsx. I'll add a helper function to generate option draft keys with the prefix-scoped format, then define a pattern to identify question fields.

I'm also creating a helper to generate the `:other` key for custom answers, and a function that checks whether a field accepts free-text input—specifically question fields that are single or multi-select. I need to import the ElicitationField type for this.

Then I'll update the existing code block to use the new pattern constant instead of the inline regex, keeping the logic the same but more maintainable.

After setting up the active question, I'm handling how the custom answer field works differently depending on whether questions are split into tabs—when they are, each question gets its own "Other" box instead of sharing a global one. I'm filtering the fields accordingly and building the visible fields based on whether we're in tab mode, then mapping over the answer fields to construct the values object.

For each field, I'm checking if it's a multi-select (which can combine both selected options and typed text), a boolean, or a single-value field (where typed text takes precedence), and pulling the appropriate stored or default values. I'm also tracking which required fields are missing.

Now I need to validate the answers, render the "Other" text box in each tab's fieldset, and update the regex check for tabbedQuestion to use a constant instead. For the textarea's label association, I'll use an id combining the prefix, field name, and `:other` suffix.

```json
  "old_string": "  const visibleFields = usesQuestionTabs\n    ? fields.filter((field) => !/^question_\\d+$/.test(field.name) || field.name === activeQuestion?.name)\n    : fields;\n  const values = Object.fromEntries(fields.map((field) => {\n    if (field.kind === \"multi_select\") {\n      const defaults = Array.isArray(field.defaultValue) ? field.defaultValue : [];\n      const selected = field.options\n        .filter((option) => {\n          const stored = workerCardManager.readElicitationDraft(optionDraftKey(prefix, field.name, option.value));\n          return stored === \"true\" || (stored === \"\" && defaults.includes(option.value));\n        })\n        .map((option) => option.value);\n      return [field.name, selected];\n    }\n    if (field.kind === \"boolean\") {\n      const stored = workerCardManager.readElicitationDraft(`${prefix}${field.name}`);\n      return [field.name, stored ? stored === \"true\" : field.defaultValue === true];\n    }\n    const stored = workerCardManager.readElicitationDraft(`${prefix}${field.name}`);\n    return [field.name, stored !== \"\" ? stored : field.defaultValue ?? \"\"];\n  })) as Record<string, ElicitationValue>;\n  const missingRequired = hasMissingRequiredElicitationField(fields, values);\n  const invalid = hasInvalidElicitationField(fields, values);",
  "new_string": "  // The agent appends a single global `customAnswer` box per request. It is not\n  // a question field, so under tabs it rendered inside every tab off one shared\n  // draft key — one \"Other\" carrying across sections. Once the questions are\n  // split into tabs each gets its own box folded into its own answer, and the\n  // global field is dropped rather than sent alongside them.\n  const customAnswerField = fields.find((field) => field.name === CUSTOM_ANSWER_FIELD) ?? null;\n  const perQuestionOther = usesQuestionTabs && customAnswerField !== null;\n  const answerFields = perQuestionOther\n    ? fields.filter((field) => field.name !== CUSTOM_ANSWER_FIELD)\n    : fields;\n  const visibleFields = usesQuestionTabs\n    ? answerFields.filter((field) => !QUESTION_FIELD_PATTERN.test(field.name) || field.name === activeQuestion?.name)\n    : answerFields;\n  const values = Object.fromEntries(answerFields.map((field) => {\n    const other = perQuestionOther && acceptsOtherText(field)\n      ? workerCardManager.readElicitationDraft(otherDraftKey(prefix, field.name)).trim()\n      : \"\";\n    if (field.kind === \"multi_select\") {\n      const defaults = Array.isArray(field.defaultValue) ? field.defaultValue : [];\n      const selected = field.options\n        .filter((option) => {\n          const stored = workerCardManager.readElicitationDraft(optionDraftKey(prefix, field.name, option.value));\n          return stored === \"true\" || (stored === \"\" && defaults.includes(option.value));\n        })\n        .map((option) => option.value);\n      // A multi-select answer is joined back into one string, so typed text can\n      // ride alongside the picked options rather than displacing them.\n      return [field.name, other ? [...selected, other] : selected];\n    }\n    if (field.kind === \"boolean\") {\n      const stored = workerCardManager.readElicitationDraft(`${prefix}${field.name}`);\n      return [field.name, stored ? stored === \"true\" : field.defaultValue === true];\n    }\n    // A single-value field holds one answer, so a typed one replaces the pick.\n    if (other) return [field.name, other];\n    const stored = workerCardManager.readElicitationDraft(`${prefix}${field.name}`);\n    return [field.name, stored !== \"\" ? stored : field.defaultValue ?? \"\"];\n  })) as Record<string, ElicitationValue>;\n  const missingRequired = hasMissingRequiredElicitationField(answerFields, values);\n  const invalid = hasInvalidElicitationField(answerFields, values);"
}
```

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-05T10:24:44.303Z

Since `window` is undefined in the test environment, I can seed draft data directly through the manager's in-memory state using `setElicitationDraft`, which bypasses the sessionStorage check. This lets me test that the single-select radio renders deselected when another draft exists for that question, validating the fold behavior.

But I'm realizing the real issue: I need to verify the fold logic itself — that the "Other" answer actually gets merged into the submitted values. Option 1 (seeding drafts and asserting the radio state) only proves the fold happens visually, not what gets sent. Option 2 would be extracting the fold into a pure function I can unit-test directly. The cleanest approach is moving this logic into `elicitation-schema.ts` where it belongs with the other pure schema utilities, then testing it there.

uuid: 3fff826a-054f-4ce7-a826-1ec7b2bd2fef
parent: 4ea015d1-04fa-4299-9d41-908c1e97ebc3

```text
+/**
+ * Fold a question's free-text "Other" answer into the value sent back for that
+ * question. A multi-select is joined into one answer agent-side, so typed text
+ * rides alongside the picked options; a single-value field can hold only one
+ * answer, so typed text replaces the pick.
+ */
+export function applyOtherAnswer(
+  field: ElicitationField,
+  value: ElicitationValue,
+  other: string,
+): ElicitationValue {
+  const text = other.trim();
+  if (!text) return value;
+  if (field.kind === "multi_select") {
+    const selected = Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];
+    return selected.includes(text) ? selected : [...selected, text];
+  }
+  return text;
+}
+
 export function buildElicitationContent(
   fields: readonly ElicitationField[],
   values: Readonly<Record<string, ElicitationValue | undefined>>,
```

## Claude · 2026-08-05T10:26:05.380Z

Done. Tests, typecheck, and lint all pass.

## What changed

**`src/components/agent-interactions/InlineElicitation.tsx`**
- `customAnswer` is now excluded from `answerFields` when the request renders as tabs, so it no longer leaks into every tab via the non-question passthrough at the old line 69.
- Each choice-bearing question tab renders its own "Other" textarea, keyed `${prefix}question_N:other` — separate draft keys, so text typed in one tab can't appear in another.
- On submit, each tab's typed text folds into *that question's* value, so it arrives as `answers[thatQuestionText]` rather than a single global `response`.

**`src/lib/acp/elicitation-schema.ts`** — new `applyOtherAnswer()` holds the fold rule: multi-select appends (the agent joins the array into one answer, so picks + typed text coexist), single-select replaces (one field, one value). Extracted rather than inlined because there's no DOM test environment here — `tests/ui` is `renderToStaticMarkup` only, so the submit path isn't otherwise reachable from a test.

The label and description come from the schema's own `customAnswer` field rather than new i18n keys, so the wording stays whatever the adapter sends and locale parity is untouched.

## Two things worth knowing

**The single-question case is unchanged.** One question means no tabs, one "Other" box, still mapped to `customAnswer` → the tool's top-level `response`. That was always correct; only the multi-question case was wrong.

**This is a workaround for an upstream limitation, not a proper fix.** `@agentclientprotocol/claude-agent-acp` builds `question_0..N` in a loop then appends exactly one `customAnswer` outside it — there is no per-question free-text channel in the schema it emits. We can route typed text through `question_N` only because `applyAskElicitationResponse` does a bare `String(value)` / `join(", ")` with no enum validation. If upstream ever starts validating responses against the declared `oneOf`, off-enum text would be rejected and this would break. The durable fix is upstream emitting per-question custom fields.

One unrelated note: `git diff` shows `src/server/conversations/{send-message,queued-messages}.ts` and `src/server/runs/recovery-*.ts` as modified. Those aren't mine — they weren't in the starting `git status` either, so something else in this workspace touched them during the session. I left them alone.

```text
diff --git a/src/server/runs/recovery-incidents.ts b/src/server/runs/recovery-incidents.ts
index 79252cc..005c8bf 100644
--- a/src/server/runs/recovery-incidents.ts
+++ b/src/server/runs/recovery-incidents.ts
@@ -9,6 +9,10 @@ export type RecoveryIncidentKind = "worker_lost" | "session_missing" | "queue_bl
 export type RecoveryIncidentStatus = "open" | "recovering" | "resolved" | "needs_user" | "failed";
 
 const OPEN_INCIDENT_STATUSES: RecoveryIncidentStatus[] = ["open", "recovering", "needs_user"];
+// `failed` belongs here even though it carries a `resolvedAt` stamp: the recovery
+// banner treats it as active, so a gave-up incident is just as visible as an open
+// one and needs the same sweep to disappear.
+const UNSETTLED_INCIDENT_STATUSES: RecoveryIncidentStatus[] = ["open", "recovering", "needs_user", "failed"];
 
 function serializeDetails(details: Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined) {
   return details ? JSON.stringify(details) : null;
@@ -253,6 +257,53 @@ export async function markRecoveryIncidentFailed(args: {
   });
 }
 
+/**
+ * A worker turn that completes normally is proof the run recovered: the session
+ * exists, the runtime answers, the credentials work, quota is not exhausted.
+ * Nothing used to act on that proof — `markRecoveryIncidentResolved` was only
+ * reachable from an automatic recovery attempt, a quota resume, or the user
+ * stopping the conversation — so a `needs_user` incident survived the very turn
+ * that disproved it and the recovery banner stayed up forever. Anything still
+ * genuinely broken is reopened by the next `openRecoveryIncident` call.
+ */
+export async function resolveRecoveryIncidentsAfterHealthyTurn(args: {
+  runId: string;
+  workerId?: string | null;
+  summary: string;
+  reason: string;
+}) {
+  const records = await db
+    .select()
+    .from(recoveryIncidents)
+    .where(and(
+      eq(recoveryIncidents.runId, args.runId),
+      inArray(recoveryIncidents.status, UNSETTLED_INCIDENT_STATUSES),
+    ));
+
+  // Scope to the worker that just proved itself, plus run-level incidents that
+  // name no worker. A healthy worker in a multi-worker implementation run says
+  // nothing about a sibling that is still lost.
+  const stale = records.filter((record) => (
+    !record.workerId || !args.workerId || record.workerId === args.workerId
+  ));
+
+  for (const incident of stale) {
+    await markRecoveryIncidentResolved({
+      incidentId: incident.id,
+      runId: args.runId,
+      workerId: incident.workerId,
+      summary: args.summary,
+      details: {
+        reason: args.reason,
+        resolvedByHealthyTurn: true,
+        previousStatus: incident.status,
+      },
+    });
+  }
+
+  return stale.length;
+}
+
 export async function listRecoveryIncidentsForRun(runId: string) {
   return db.select().from(recoveryIncidents).where(eq(recoveryIncidents.runId, runId));
 }

   if (state.kind === "healthy" || state.kind === "recovering") {
+    // A silent early return here left incidents from an earlier failure hanging
+    // open long after the run went healthy again, which is what kept the recovery
+    // banner on screen. `recovering` is genuinely still in flight, so only
```

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-05T15:15:16.446Z

The banner component reads the recovery state's attempt count and pulls the title from recovery utilities, with the error message coming from `selectedRun.lastError`. I need to trace where `recoveryState` originates from in the component hierarchy.

Looking at this, `recoveryState` appears to be computed server-side through something like `computeRunRecoveryState`, which derives from the run's status and `lastError` rather than from the incident table itself. This would explain why clearing incidents doesn't clear the banner — it's actually driven by the run's failed status and error field, not by incident rows.

## Claude tool result: toolu_015dQyvGE3qkUP4aSEYVh8ME · 2026-08-05T15:15:16.476Z

```text
import type { RecoveryIncidentRecord, RunRecoveryState } from "./types";

export function recoveryTone(state: RunRecoveryState | null | undefined) {
  if (!state) return "muted" as const;
  if (state.status === "failed") return "error" as const;
  if (state.status === "needs_user" || state.kind === "needs_recovery") return "warning" as const;
  if (state.status === "recovering") return "active" as const;
  return "warning" as const;
}

export function recoveryTitleKey(state: RunRecoveryState | null | undefined) {
  if (!state) return "recovery.notice.title.default";
  if (state.kind === "quota_waiting") return "recovery.notice.title.quotaWaiting";
  if (state.status === "recovering") return "recovery.notice.title.recovering";
  if (state.status === "failed") return "recovery.notice.title.failed";
  if (state.kind === "queue_blocked") return "recovery.notice.title.queueBlocked";
  if (state.kind === "lost_worker_resumable") return "recovery.notice.title.workerDisconnected";
  if (state.kind === "lost_worker_rerunnable") return "recovery.notice.title.workerDisconnected";
  return "recovery.notice.title.needsRecovery";
}

export function recoveryDescriptionKey(state: RunRecoveryState | null | undefined) {
  if (!state) return "recovery.notice.description.empty";
  if (state.kind === "quota_waiting") return "recovery.notice.description.quotaWaiting";
  if (state.message?.trim()) return state.message.trim();
  if (state.lastError?.trim()) return state.lastError.trim();
  if (state.status === "recovering") return "recovery.notice.description.recovering";
  if (state.kind === "lost_worker_resumable") return "recovery.notice.description.lostWorkerResumable";
  if (state.kind === "lost_worker_rerunnable") return "recovery.notice.description.lostWorkerRerunnable";
  return "recovery.notice.description.needsRecovery";
}

export function latestRelevantRecoveryIncident(incidents: RecoveryIncidentRecord[]) {
  return [...incidents].sort((a, b) => {
    const updatedDelta = new Date(b.updatedAt).getTime() - new Date(a.updatedAt).getTime();
    return updatedDelta !== 0 ? updatedDelta : b.id.localeCompare(a.id);
  })[0] ?? null;
}
```

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-05T15:16:35.078Z

I've found the healthy-turn hooks in the right places—after normal ask responses and in the queued messages handler. But the incident is still marked as needing user input, so I'm considering why the code might not be executing. These are uncommitted changes, and while Next.js hot-reloads server code, OmniHarness runs separate runner and bridge processes that might not be picking up the updates.

Looking at the timestamps more carefully: the incident was updated at 1785876470 seconds, and the run at 1785942954 seconds—that's about 18.5 hours later. The run status is still "running" despite all that time passing, which suggests turns have definitely completed since the incident was created. So either `markRecoveryIncidentResolved` has some filtering logic that's preventing it from resolving, or the user's current session might actually be this very run I'm investigating.

The key thing to check now is when those uncommitted changes were actually written—if they're recent but the incident is 18 hours old, then turns have happened without exercising the fix. I need to look at the file modification times and compare them against the run's updated_at timestamp, plus verify whether the server process is even running the current code by checking the runner lock file.
